      :host {

        --tooltip-left: var(--tool-tip-position-left, 0);
        padding: var(--overlay-paddingBlock-condensed) var(--overlay-padding-condensed) !important;
        font: var(--text-body-shorthand-small);
        color: var(--tooltip-fgColor, var(--fgColor-onEmphasis)) !important;

        text-decoration: none;
        text-shadow: none;
        text-transform: none;
        letter-spacing: normal;
        word-wrap: break-word;
        white-space: pre;
        background: var(--tooltip-bgColor, var(--bgColor-emphasis)) !important;
        border-radius: var(--borderRadius-medium);
        border: 0 !important;
        opacity: 0;
        max-width: min(var(--overlay-width-small), 100vw);
        word-wrap: break-word;
        white-space: normal;
        width: max-content !important;
        inset: var(--tooltip-top) auto auto var(--tooltip-left) !important;
        overflow: visible !important;
        text-wrap: balance;
      }

      :host(:is(.tooltip-n, .tooltip-nw, .tooltip-ne)) {
        --tooltip-top: calc(var(--tool-tip-position-top, 0) - var(--overlay-offset, 0.25rem));
        --tooltip-left: var(--tool-tip-position-left);
      }

      :host(:is(.tooltip-s, .tooltip-sw, .tooltip-se)) {
        --tooltip-top: calc(var(--tool-tip-position-top, 0) + var(--overlay-offset, 0.25rem));
        --tooltip-left: var(--tool-tip-position-left);
      }

      :host(.tooltip-w) {
        --tooltip-top: var(--tool-tip-position-top);
        --tooltip-left: calc(var(--tool-tip-position-left, 0) - var(--overlay-offset, 0.25rem));
      }

      :host(.tooltip-e) {
        --tooltip-top: var(--tool-tip-position-top);
        --tooltip-left: calc(var(--tool-tip-position-left, 0) + var(--overlay-offset, 0.25rem));
      }

      :host:after{
        position: absolute;
        display: block;
        right: 0;
        left: 0;
        height: var(--overlay-offset, 0.25rem);
        content: "";
      }

      :host(.tooltip-s):after,
      :host(.tooltip-se):after,
      :host(.tooltip-sw):after {
        bottom: 100%
      }

      :host(.tooltip-n):after,
      :host(.tooltip-ne):after,
      :host(.tooltip-nw):after {
        top: 100%;
      }

      @keyframes tooltip-appear {
        from {
          opacity: 0;
        }
        to {
          opacity: 1;
        }
      }

      :host(:popover-open),
      :host(:popover-open):before {
        animation-name: tooltip-appear;
        animation-duration: .1s;
        animation-fill-mode: forwards;
        animation-timing-function: ease-in;
      }

      :host(.\:popover-open) {
        animation-name: tooltip-appear;
        animation-duration: .1s;
        animation-fill-mode: forwards;
        animation-timing-function: ease-in;
      }

      @media (forced-colors: active) {
        :host {
          outline: solid 1px transparent;
        }

        :host:before {
          display: none;
        }
      }

BlobContent-module__markdownBlob__T8jpG" data-hpc="true"><article class="markdown-body entry-content container-lg" itemprop="text"><div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">EduApp REST API</h1><a id="user-content-eduapp-rest-api" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: EduApp REST API" href="https://github.com/a8anassis/edu9-rest-app-pro/blob/main/README.md#eduapp-rest-api"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">A Spring Boot REST API for managing teachers and users in an educational context, featuring JWT-based authentication, role-based access control, soft-delete, file uploads, and async report generation.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Tech Stack</h2><a id="user-content-tech-stack" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Tech Stack" href="https://github.com/a8anassis/edu9-rest-app-pro/blob/main/README.md#tech-stack"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Java 21</strong> / <strong>Spring Boot 3</strong></li>
<li><strong>Spring Security</strong> — stateless JWT authentication</li>
<li><strong>Spring Data JPA</strong> + <strong>Flyway</strong> — schema migrations, no DDL auto-update</li>
<li><strong>MySQL 8+</strong></li>
<li><strong>Lombok</strong>, <strong>Jakarta Validation</strong></li>
<li><strong>OpenAPI / Swagger UI</strong> — interactive API docs</li>
<li><strong>Logback</strong> — structured logging with MDC (request-scoped context)</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Requirements</h2><a id="user-content-requirements" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Requirements" href="https://github.com/a8anassis/edu9-rest-app-pro/blob/main/README.md#requirements"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Java 21</li>
<li>MySQL 8+</li>
<li>Gradle (wrapper included)</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Database Setup</h2><a id="user-content-database-setup" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Database Setup" href="https://github.com/a8anassis/edu9-rest-app-pro/blob/main/README.md#database-setup"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Create a MySQL database and user:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-sql notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto"><pre><span class="pl-k">CREATE</span> <span class="pl-k">DATABASE</span> <span class="pl-en">schoolapp9csrpro</span> CHARACTER <span class="pl-k">SET</span> utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;
<span class="pl-k">CREATE</span> <span class="pl-k">USER</span> '<span class="pl-en">user9</span>'@<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>localhost<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> IDENTIFIED BY <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>12345<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>;
<span class="pl-k">GRANT</span> ALL PRIVILEGES <span class="pl-k">ON</span> schoolapp9csrpro.<span class="pl-k">*</span> TO <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>user9<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>@<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>localhost<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>;
FLUSH PRIVILEGES;</pre><div class="zeroclipboard-container">
    <clipboard-copy aria-label="Copy" class="ClipboardButton btn btn-invisible js-clipboard-copy m-2 p-0 d-flex flex-justify-center flex-items-center" data-copy-feedback="Copied!" data-tooltip-direction="w" value="CREATE DATABASE schoolapp9csrpro CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;
CREATE USER &#39;user9&#39;@&#39;localhost&#39; IDENTIFIED BY &#39;12345&#39;;
GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON schoolapp9csrpro.* TO &#39;user9&#39;@&#39;localhost&#39;;
FLUSH PRIVILEGES;" tabindex="0" role="button">
      <svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" data-view-component="true" class="octicon octicon-copy js-clipboard-copy-icon">

<p dir="auto">Flyway runs all migrations automatically on startup from <code>src/main/resources/db/migration/</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Configuration</h2><a id="user-content-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Configuration" href="https://github.com/a8anassis/edu9-rest-app-pro/blob/main/README.md#configuration"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The active profile is <code>dev</code> by default. Dev settings live in <code>src/main/resources/application-dev.properties</code>.</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table data-catalyst=""><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Property</th>
<th>Default (dev)</th>
<th>Override via</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>DB host</td>
<td><code>localhost</code></td>
<td><code>MYSQL_HOST</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>DB port</td>
<td><code>3306</code></td>
<td><code>MYSQL_PORT</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>DB name</td>
<td><code>schoolapp9csrpro</code></td>
<td><code>MYSQL_DB</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>DB user</td>
<td><code>user9</code></td>
<td><code>MYSQL_USER</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>DB password</td>
<td><code>12345</code></td>
<td><code>MYSQL_PASSWORD</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>JWT secret</td>
<td><em>(dev value)</em></td>
<td><code>app.security.secret-key</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>JWT expiration</td>
<td><code>10800000</code> ms (3h)</td>
<td><code>app.security.jwt-expiration</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>CORS origins</td>
<td><em>(dev value)</em></td>
<td><code>allowed.origins</code> (comma-separated)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>BCrypt strength</td>
<td><code>12</code></td>
<td><code>security.bcrypt.strength</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Production</strong>: never commit real secrets. Inject <code>app.security.secret-key</code> via environment variable.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Build &amp; Run</h2><a id="user-content-build--run" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Build &amp; Run" href="https://github.com/a8anassis/edu9-rest-app-pro/blob/main/README.md#build--run"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto"><pre>./gradlew build          <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Compile and package</span>
./gradlew bootRun        <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Start with the dev profile</span>
./gradlew <span class="pl-c1">test</span>           <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Run all tests</span>
./gradlew <span class="pl-c1">test</span> --tests <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>gr.aueb.cf.eduapp.SomeTest<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Run a single test</span>
./gradlew clean          <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Clean build output</span></pre><div class="zeroclipboard-container">
    <clipboard-copy aria-label="Copy" class="ClipboardButton btn btn-invisible js-clipboard-copy m-2 p-0 d-flex flex-justify-center flex-items-center" data-copy-feedback="Copied!" data-tooltip-direction="w" value="./gradlew build          # Compile and package
./gradlew bootRun        # Start with the dev profile
./gradlew test           # Run all tests
./gradlew test --tests &quot;gr.aueb.cf.eduapp.SomeTest&quot;  # Run a single test
./gradlew clean          # Clean build output" tabindex="0" role="button">
      <svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" data-view-component="true" class="octicon octicon-copy js-clipboard-copy-icon">

<p dir="auto">The server starts on port <strong>8080</strong>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">API Overview</h2><a id="user-content-api-overview" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: API Overview" href="https://github.com/a8anassis/edu9-rest-app-pro/blob/main/README.md#api-overview"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Authentication</h3><a id="user-content-authentication" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Authentication" href="https://github.com/a8anassis/edu9-rest-app-pro/blob/main/README.md#authentication"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table data-catalyst=""><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Method</th>
<th>Endpoint</th>
<th>Auth</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>POST</td>
<td><code>/auth/authenticate</code></td>
<td>Public</td>
<td>Log in and receive a JWT</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>

<markdown-accessiblity-table data-catalyst=""><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Method</th>
<th>Endpoint</th>
<th>Auth</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>POST</td>
<td><code>/teachers</code></td>
<td>Public</td>
<td>Create a teacher</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>GET</td>
<td><code>/teachers</code></td>
<td>Bearer</td>
<td>List teachers (paginated + filtered)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>GET</td>
<td><code>/teachers/{uuid}</code></td>
<td>Bearer</td>
<td>Get teacher by UUID</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>PUT</td>
<td><code>/teachers/{uuid}</code></td>
<td>Bearer</td>
<td>Update a teacher</td>
</tr>

<td>DELETE</td>

</tr>

<td>POST</td>

</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Pagination defaults:</strong> <code>page=0</code>, <code>size=5</code>. Pass <code>page</code>, <code>size</code>, and <code>sort</code> as query params.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Eligible Report (async)</h3><a id="user-content-eligible-report-async" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Eligible Report (async)" href="https://github.com/a8anassis/edu9-rest-app-pro/blob/main/README.md#eligible-report-async"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table data-catalyst=""><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Method</th>
<th>Endpoint</th>
<th>Auth</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>POST</td>
<td><code>/eligible/report</code></td>
<td>Public</td>
<td>Start async report generation; returns <code>jobId</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>GET</td>
<td><code>/eligible/report/{jobId}</code></td>
<td>Public</td>
<td>Poll for report status/result</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Password Policy</h2><a id="user-content-password-policy" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Password Policy" href="https://github.com/a8anassis/edu9-rest-app-pro/blob/main/README.md#password-policy"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Passwords must be at least 8 characters and contain:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>One digit</li>
<li>One lowercase letter</li>
<li>One uppercase letter</li>
<li>One special character (<code>!@#$%^&amp;+=</code>)</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Error Responses</h2><a id="user-content-error-responses" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Error Responses" href="https://github.com/a8anassis/edu9-rest-app-pro/blob/main/README.md#error-responses"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">All errors return a JSON body. Validation failures include per-field messages.</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table data-catalyst=""><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>HTTP Status</th>
<th>Cause</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>400</td>
<td>Validation error</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>401</td>
<td>Missing or invalid JWT</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>403</td>
<td>Insufficient permissions</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>404</td>
<td>Resource not found</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>409</td>
<td>Resource already exists</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>500</td>
<td>File upload failure or unexpected error</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Data Model</h2><a id="user-content-data-model" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Data Model" href="https://github.com/a8anassis/edu9-rest-app-pro/blob/main/README.md#data-model"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<p dir="auto">All entities extend <code>AbstractEntity</code> and support <strong>soft delete</strong> (<code>deleted</code> flag + <code>deletedAt</code>). Records are never hard-deleted. External identifiers are UUIDs — internal <code>id</code> columns are never exposed in the API.</p>
